### [96] [Universal Interatomic Potentials as Configuration-Space Generators for One-Shot and Iterative Fine-Tuning of Ab Initio-Accurate Material-Specific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23214)
*Jonas Hänseroth,Aaron Flötotto,Christian Dreßler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种通用机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在涉及反应过程时的定量材料建模能力，发现其在标准基准上表现优异但无法准确预测目标可观测物理量（如反应、输运、高势垒过程）。为此提出实用替代方案：利用通用MLIP生成长分子动力学轨迹，对构象进行降采样并用DFT重新标注，训练或微调材料专用MLIP。实验表明2000个DFT重算结构足以获得高精度模型，最具挑战的MoS₂硫空位跳跃仅需600次DFT计算，整个流程可在三天内完成1纳秒的ab initio质量轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势在涉及反应事件时是否能够可靠进行定量材料建模尚不明确，现有标准基准测试无法保证其在实际目标观测物理量上的预测准确性，亟需系统评估和实用解决方案。

Method: 1) 跨七个化学多样性系统，对五种通用MLIP进行系统比较；2) 提出"通用MLIP生成轨迹→DFT重新标注→微调或训练专用模型"的工作流；3) 针对最具挑战的MoS₂硫空位跳跃案例，引入迭代自训练策略渐进精炼构象空间。

Result: 1) 通用MLIP在标准基准测试中表现优异，但无法可靠预测反应、输运和高势垒过程；2) 2000个DFT重算结构通常足以获得高精度微调或从头训练模型；3) MoS₂硫空位跳跃仅需600次DFT计算即可恢复势能面；4) 整个流程可在三天内生成1纳秒的ab initio质量轨迹（含训练数据生成和模型创建）。

Conclusion: 通用MLIPs不适合直接用于涉及反应事件的定量材料建模，但可作为高效的构象空间生成器。结合DFT重新标注和材料专用模型微调/训练的混合工作流，能够在有限计算成本下实现高精度、长时间尺度的ab initio质量分子动力学模拟。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are rapidly becoming general-purpose tools for atomistic simulation, but their role in quantitative materials modeling when reactive events are involved remains unsettled. We compare five universal MLIPs across seven chemically diverse systems and find that strong performance on standard benchmarks does not guarantee accurate predictions of target observables. In particular, zero-shot models do not reliably reproduce reactive, transport, or high-barrier processes, exemplified here in particular by the sulfur-vacancy jump in MoS$_2$. We therefore propose a practical alternative: universal MLIPs are used to generate long molecular dynamics trajectories, the resulting configurations are sub-sampled and relabeled with DFT, and material-specific MLIPs are subsequently trained or fine-tuned on the resulting first-principles datasets. This workflow converts universal models into efficient configuration-space generators while retaining ab initio reference labels for training. Across the tested systems, $2{,}000$ DFT-recalculated structures are often sufficient to obtain accurate fine-tuned or trained-from-scratch models. For the most challenging case, iterative self-training progressively refines the sampled configuration space and recovers the DFT MoS$_2$ potential energy profile with only $600$ first-principles calculations in total. The resulting workflow enables the generation of $1$ ns ab initio-quality trajectories - including training data generation and model creation - within three days.

</details>


### [97] [Step-Edge Passivation and Quantitative Raman Mapping of Transfer Quality in Aligned Graphene Nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23304)
*Dominik Lüthi,Rimah Darawish,Klaus Müllen,Roman Fasel,Gabriela Borin Barin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Au(788)台阶边缘通过chevron-GNRs钝化来改善对齐的9-AGNRs转移的策略，建立了自动化大面积Raman分析框架用于量化转移质量，发现chevron钝化虽能局部改变生长构型，但未能实现可重复的高质量转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐的石墨烯纳米带从金属生长表面转移到器件兼容平台是纳米带电子学的核心瓶颈，当前缺乏有效的转移策略。

Method: 利用扫描隧道显微镜研究chevron-GNRs在Au(788)台阶边缘的钝化行为，并建立基于G模和径向呼吸模的自动化大面积Raman分析框架，实现像素级分类和转移质量量化评估。

Result: STM显示chevron-GNRs优先占据台阶边缘，将9-AGNRs推向台面中心；Raman映射揭示转移高度不均匀，仅有一个高产率异常样本，大多数样品转移产率远低于100%。

Conclusion: chevron钝化虽能局部改变9-AGNRs的生长构型，但尚无法实现可重复的高质量转移；所建立的Raman分析框架可作为优化GNR转移策略的定量基准。

Abstract: The transfer of aligned graphene nanoribbons from metallic growth surfaces to device-compatible platforms remains a central bottleneck for nanoribbon electronics. Here, we investigate step-edge passivation of vicinal Au(788) by chevron-GNRs as a strategy to improve the transfer of aligned 9-armchair graphene nanoribbons. Scanning tunneling microscopy reveals that chevron-GNRs preferentially occupy step-edges, effectively acting as passivators that displace 9-AGNRs toward terrace centers, thereby altering their local growth configuration. To quantify transfer performance, we establish an automated large-area Raman analysis framework that enables pixel-wise classification based on the G mode and the radial breathing-like mode (RBLM). This approach provides a robust and scalable metric for assessing both transfer coverage and local ribbon integrity across macroscopic areas. Raman mapping uncovers strongly inhomogeneous transfer, characterized by extended regions with no detectable GNR signal and pronounced spatial variability in the RBLM-to-G intensity ratio. Transfer quality varies substantially across the sample series, with only a single high-yield outlier and most samples remaining well below 100% transfer yield. These results demonstrate that while chevron passivation locally modifies the growth configuration of 9-AGNRs on Au(788), it does not yet yield reproducible, high-quality transfer of intact aligned ribbons. The presented Raman-based analysis framework establishes a quantitative benchmark for the systematic optimization of GNR transfer strategies.

</details>


### [98] [Atomic-scale theory of robust out-of-plane ferroelectricity in ultrathin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23508)
*Fengbo Yuan,Yujia Teng,Karin M. Rabe,Yubo Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个原子尺度的理论框架，通过考虑薄膜终止层的功函数，揭示了超薄铁电薄膜中“自极化”和“终止层可切换角色”效应与“特征结构”的强关联，解释了HfO₂基和铋基氧化物在超薄极限下仍能保持稳定铁电性的机制，并阐明了顶电极的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观铁电理论认为薄膜厚度减小会抑制铁电性，但近期实验发现某些材料（HfO₂基氧化物和铋基氧化物）在超薄极限下仍表现出稳健铁电性，现有理论无法解释这一现象，亟需建立原子尺度的理论框架。

Method: 通过分析薄膜终止层的功函数，建立了原子尺度的理论框架，提出了“自极化”效应和“终止层可切换角色”效应，并揭示了这些效应与薄膜“特征结构”之间的强关联。

Result: 发现超薄铁电性源于终止层的自极化和可切换角色效应，这些效应与薄膜的特征结构密切相关；同时揭示了顶电极在稳定该类材料超薄极限下铁电性的重要性。

Conclusion: 该工作为超薄薄膜铁电性提供了全面的理论框架，为下一代纳米器件的设计提供了基础性指导。

Abstract: Ferroelectricity in ultrathin films, characterized by robust switchable out-of-plane polarization, is key to next-generation nanoelectronics. Although the macroscopic theory of ferroelectricity suggests that ferroelectricity is inevitably suppressed as the film thickness decreases, recent studies have demonstrated robust ultra thin-film ferroelectricity, for certain ferroelectric materials, specifically HfO$_2$-based oxides and bismuth-based oxides. In this work, we develop an atomic-scale theoretical framework for understanding ferroelectricity in this limiting regime. By considering the work function of the termination layers of the film, we find that robust ferroelectricity arises from ``self-polarizing'' and ``switchable role of the termination layer'' effects strongly correlated to the ``characteristic structure.'' This theory also provides further insights on the importance of top electrodes in stabilizing ferroelectricity for this class of materials in the ultrathin limit. This work aims to develop a comprehensive theoretical framework for thin-film ferroelectricity, providing fundamental insights that can guide the design of next-generation nanoscale devices.

</details>


### [99] [INCARBench: A Benchmark for Scientific Configuration in VASP INCAR by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23571)
*Bin Shao,Jixiang Li,Xinyue Zhang,Baishun Yang,Zhiyang Liu,Weichao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了INCARBench基准，用于评估大语言模型在VASP计算输入配置上的能力。评估19个模型发现，前沿模型在语义和政策准确性上表现较好，但在任务关键正确性上显著偏低，错误主要集中在DFT+U、磁性和关联材料等物理耦合设置中。修复评估表明，修正错误与保留已有有效配置是两种不同的能力，后者仍是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正越来越多地被集成到第一性原理计算工作流中，但其配置科学计算的能力尚不明确，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 提出INCARBench基准，通过配置生成和配置修复两类任务，评估19种大语言模型在VASP输入参数配置上的表现，并进行失败分析和能力区分。

Result: 前沿模型在语义和政策准确性上较高，但任务关键正确性显著偏低；参数级正确性不等于科学有效配置；错误集中在DFT+U、磁性和关联材料等物理耦合场景；修正错误与保留有效配置是两种不同能力。

Conclusion: 科学配置可被量化为大语言模型的一种可测量能力，为开发更可靠的计算材料科学AI系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being integrated into first-principles computational workflows, yet their ability to configure scientific calculations remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce INCARBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on input configuration for the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP) through both configuration generation and repair tasks. Evaluating 19 model configurations reveals substantial capability differences among current frontier models. While several models achieve high semantic and policy accuracy, task-critical correctness remains substantially lower, demonstrating that parameter-level correctness does not necessarily imply scientifically valid configurations. Failure analysis shows that errors concentrate in physically coupled settings involving DFT+$U$, magnetism, and correlated materials, where multiple constraints must be satisfied simultaneously. Repair evaluation further reveals that correcting incorrect settings and preserving already-valid configurations are distinct capabilities, with configuration preservation remaining a major challenge. These findings establish scientific configuration as a measurable capability of large language models and provide a foundation for developing more reliable AI systems for computational materials science.

</details>


### [100] [Data analysis methods for powder x-ray diffraction intensity under laser-driven dynamic compression at Omega and NIF laser facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23602)
*Marius Millot,Federica Coppari,Amy Lazicki,Jon H. Eggert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在激光驱动动态压缩下粉末X射线衍射（PXRD）平台的数据分析方法改进，包括使用原位参考标定衍射强度、不同X射线源数据对比以及热阻尼效应的校正，并以金刚石在1 TPa冲击压缩下的数据为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升激光驱动动态压缩PXRD平台（如Omega的PXRDIP和NIF的TARDIS）的数据分析保真度，解决衍射强度测定的准确性问题。

Method: 提出了利用准直针孔或样品包中未压缩材料层作为原位参考来标定XRD强度的方法；详细阐述了不同X射线源数据的对比方式；以及如何校正冲击压缩热材料与室温参考材料之间因热阻尼效应导致的衍射信号差异。这些方法被集成到一个数据分析包中。

Result: 以金刚石在近1 TPa冲击压缩下的数据为例，展示了上述方法在PXRD强度测定保真度提升方面的应用效果。

Conclusion: 所发展的数据分析方法能够显著提高激光驱动动态压缩PXRD实验的强度测定精度，为极端条件下材料响应的研究提供了更可靠的数据处理手段。

Abstract: Powder x-ray diffraction (PXRD) under laser-driven dynamic compression is a powerful tool to investigate material response to extreme pressure, temperature and strain rates. Robust PXRD platforms have been developed at kJ and MJ laser facilities worldwide including the Powder X-Ray Diffraction Image Plate (PXRDIP) at the Omega Laser Facility at the Laboratory for Laser Energetics (LLE) and the TARget Diffraction In Situ (TARDIS) at the National Ignition Facility (NIF). Here we present further developments of data analysis methods focused towards improving the fidelity of the PXRD intensity determination for these platforms. We illustrate these methods by discussing how they can be implemented in a data analysis package and applied to shock compression data on diamond near 1 TPa. We discuss using the XRD signal from the collimating pinhole or a layer of un-compressed material in the sample package as \textit{ in-situ} references for XRD intensity. We detail how to compare data collected with different x-ray sources and how to account for thermal damping of XRD signal when comparing XRD from a shock-compressed, hot material with the reference material at ambient.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Complexity Inequalities for Quantum Subsystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20790)
*Pawel Caputa,Giuseppe Di Giulio,Tran Quang Loc*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了多部分量子系统中约化密度矩阵的子系统复杂度的组合性质，引入三部分复杂度和复杂度间隙两个量，并在全息、高斯多体态和Krylov有效框架三种方法下分析其符号结构。发现三部分复杂度符号不定，而复杂度间隙在所有示例中符号确定但依赖具体定义，暗示其可作为子系统复杂度不等式层次结构的候选基本模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受全息熵锥在约束具有经典引力对偶态纠缠结构中的作用的启发，探索与约化密度矩阵相关的子系统复杂度的组合关系，旨在建立类似熵锥的子系统复杂度层次结构。

Method: 定义了两个量：三部分复杂度（受三部分信息启发）和复杂度间隙（表征量子态中超出其组成部分的新兴复杂度）。在三种方法中研究：全息中采用复杂度=体积方案（AdS时空）；高斯多体态中使用Fisher-Rao子系统复杂度；进一步开发了基于Krylov空间的约化密度矩阵有效框架，并在少量子比特和相干态动力学中测试。

Result: 三种方法中，三部分复杂度一般不具有确定的符号。复杂度间隙在所有分析示例中具有确定符号，但该符号依赖于子系统复杂度的具体定义方式。

Conclusion: 复杂度间隙可作为构建子系统复杂度不等式层次结构的自然候选基本模块。

Abstract: Motivated by the role of the holographic entropy cone in constraining the entanglement structure of states with classical gravitational duals, we investigate combinations of subsystem complexities associated with reduced density matrices in multipartite quantum systems. Focusing on subsystems composed of three disjoint regions, we introduce two quantities: a tripartite complexity, inspired by the tripartite information, and a complexity gap, designed to characterize emergent complexity in the full quantum state beyond that of its constituents. We study the sign structure of these quantities in three selected approaches to subsystem complexity. In holography, we employ the complexity=volume proposal in AdS spacetimes; for Gaussian many-body states, we use Fisher-Rao subsystem complexity; and we further develop a Krylov-space inspired, effective framework for reduced density matrices, which we test in few-qubit systems and coherent-state dynamics. Across all three approaches, we find that the tripartite complexity is not sign-definite in general. By contrast, the complexity gap exhibits a definite sign in every example we analyze, although the sign itself depends on the underlying notion of subsystem complexity. Our results suggest that the complexity gap could be a natural candidate building block for a prospective hierarchy of subsystem complexity inequalities.

</details>


### [102] [$N=1$ Supersymmetry, Weil-Petersson Volume Recursion, and a Spectral Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20796)
*Clifford V. Johnson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明Stanford-Witten-Norbury关于N=1超黎曼曲面模空间的Weil-Petersson体积递归可从谱曲线直接导出，并由此推导出另一种体积递归，其区别在于Ramond信息位于初始数据还是递归核中，后者具有几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Stanford-Witten-Norbury体积递归与谱曲线之间的直接联系，并探索不同的递归形式及其几何含义。

Method: 从已导出的谱曲线出发，通过拓扑递归计算拉普拉斯变换，推导出体积递归关系，并比较两种不同形式（Ramond信息在初始数据中 vs. 在递归核中）。

Result: 成功证明了SWN体积递归可直接从谱曲线导出，并给出了另一种等价的体积递归形式。

Conclusion: 两种递归的区别在于Ramond信息的配置方式，其中一种具有更直接的几何解释，为进一步理解超黎曼曲面模空间的几何提供了新视角。

Abstract: The Stanford-Witten-Norbury generalization of Mirzakhani's volume recursion computes $V^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$, the Weil-Petersson volumes of the moduli space of $N=1$ supersymmetric Riemann surfaces of genus $g$ with $n$ Neveu-Schwarz boundaries of geodesic lengths $b_i$ ($i{=}1,\ldots,n$), and $2m$ Ramond punctures. Recently, a spectral curve has been derived that allows their Laplace transforms $W^{(2m)}_{g,n}(\{{\hat z}_i\})$ to be computed using topological recursion. We prove that the Stanford-Witten-Norbury volume recursion is directly derivable from the spectral curve. An alternative volume recursion can also be derived from it. The difference comes from whether the Ramond information is in the initial data, or in the volume recursion's kernels. The latter invites a geometrical understanding.

</details>


### [103] [Renormalization effects fade away during inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20915)
*Christian Durán Romero,Luis J. Garay,Mercedes Martín-Benito,Rita B. Neves*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明通货膨胀动态抑制了整个重整化区域：超哈勃扰动在跨越视界后冻结，而重整化贡献在暴胀期间迅速衰减。因此，可观测的原初谱面对重整化模糊性表现出显著的稳健性，为标准暴胀预言在可观测尺度上的可靠性提供了有力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来的担忧：原始暴胀功率谱的重整化可能使紫外效应显著改变宇宙学可观测量的理论预言。

Method: 分析了暴胀动力学对重整化贡献的抑制作用：超哈勃扰动在视界穿越后冻结，而重整化贡献在暴胀期间快速衰减。

Result: 可观测的原初功率谱对重整化模糊性高度不敏感，标准暴胀预言在可观测尺度上具有稳健性。

Conclusion: 标准暴胀预言在重整化下是稳健可靠的，无需担忧紫外效应会显著改变宇宙学可观测信号。

Abstract: The renormalization of the primordial inflationary power spectrum has long raised the possibility that ultraviolet effects could significantly alter predictions for cosmological observables. We demonstrate that inflation dynamically suppresses the entire renormalization sector: while super-Hubble perturbations freeze after horizon crossing, renormalization contributions decay rapidly during inflation. As a consequence, the observable primordial spectrum is remarkably insensitive to renormalization ambiguities, providing strong evidence for the robustness under renormalization of standard inflationary predictions at observable scales.

</details>


### [104] [Dark bubbles, dark dimensions and fat gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20942)
*Ulf Danielsson,Suvendu Giri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 暗黑气泡模型利用de Sitter沼泽地猜想中的不稳定性来解释正宇宙学常数，自然预言了微米级暗维度的存在，并导致引力在微米尺度减弱，从而同时实现了暗维度提议和Sundrum的胖引力子场景。模型还预言弦尺度在数十TeV量级及可测量的正空间曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释正宇宙学常数的存在，并将de Sitter沼泽地猜想的不稳定性转化为宇宙加速膨胀的必然性。

Method: 利用暗黑气泡模型，通过建立宇宙学尺度、引力尺度、弦尺度和高维尺度之间的独特层级关系，并引入微米级暗维度。

Result: 模型预言了微米大小暗维度的存在、引力在微米距离处减弱、弦尺度约数十TeV以及可观测的正空间曲率。

Conclusion: 暗黑气泡模型为暗维度提议和胖引力子场景提供了具体实现，对引力和宇宙学有重要启示。

Abstract: The dark bubble model explains the existence of a positive cosmological constant by making explicit use of the instabilities underlying the de Sitter swampland conjectures to make the accelerated expansion of the universe inevitable. A distinctive consequence of the construction is a unique hierarchy connecting cosmological, gravitational, string, and higher-dimensional scales. In particular, the model naturally predicts the existence of a dark dimension of micron size, an idea that has been argued for on independent grounds in the literature. The same framework also predicts a weakening of gravity at distances of order the dark-dimension scale, leading to a fading of the gravitational force at micron distances. We argue that the dark bubble therefore provides a concrete realization of both the dark dimension proposal and Sundrum's fat graviton scenario, in which gravity effectively ceases to probe shorter distances. Additional predictions include a string scale of order tens of TeV and a measurable positive spatial curvature of the universe. We review these key aspects of the model, discuss their implications for gravity and cosmology, and highlight its key predictions.

</details>


### [105] [Linear Growth of Holographic Time-like Entanglement Entropy and Kasner exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21079)
*Zi-Hao Li,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文采用分段极值曲面的几何方案，研究渐近AdS黑洞中类时间纠缠熵(TEE)在晚期(τ₀→∞)的线性增长。通过假设类空奇点附近的Kasner几何并利用零能量条件，解析证明事件视界内的一个临界极值曲面A_c完全支配了TEE的晚期线性增长。数值结果表明，真空Schwarzschild-AdS几何对实部增长率设置上界，对虚部设置下界。这些边界在静态平面对称情况和主导能量条件下被证明，并推测在更一般情形下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类时间纠缠熵(TEE)的全息字典仍存在争议，其晚期行为与黑洞内部几何的关系尚不清晰。本文旨在通过具体几何方案，揭示TEE晚期增长与黑洞内部奇点附近几何结构之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用分段极值曲面作为TEE的全息方案，结合Kasner几何近似和零能量条件进行解析分析，并利用Einstein-标量理论的数值结果进行验证。

Result: (1) 事件视界内的临界极值曲面A_c完全支配TEE的晚期线性增长；(2) 真空Schwarzschild-AdS几何为TEE实部增长率设置上界，为虚部设置下界；(3) 在静态平面对称和主导能量条件下证明这些边界成立。

Conclusion: TEE的晚期行为受黑洞内部几何的严格约束，真空Schwarzschild-AdS几何提供的边界可能具有普遍性。

Abstract: The holographic time-like entanglement entropy (TEE) extends entanglement to time-like boundary subregions. While its definitive holographic dictionary remains debated, one concrete proposal utilizes piece-wise extremal surfaces. In this work, we adopt this geometric prescription as an exploratory framework to holographically investigate the late-time ($τ_0\to \infty$) growth of TEE in asymptotically AdS black holes with a space-like singularity and no inner horizon. By assuming a Kasner geometry near the space-like singularity and using null energy condition, we analytically show that a critical extremal surface $\mathcal{A}_c$ inside the event horizon completely governs the late-time linear growth of the TEE. This result suggests that the late-time behavior of TEE is tightly constrained by the geometry of black hole interiors. Using numerical results from Einstein-scalar theory, we find a robust behavior: the vacuum Schwarzschild-AdS geometry sets an upper bound on the growth rate of the real part and a lower bound on the imaginary part. We prove these bounds in static planar symmetric case under dominant energy condition and conjecture that it should be true in more general cases.

</details>


### [106] [The Entanglement Wedge Polygon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21081)
*Kosei Fujiki,Jonathan Harper,Tadashi Takayanagi,Nicolò Zenoni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文定义了纠缠楔多边形（EWP），即全息时空中特定余维-1区域，并研究了其在AdS时空中的性质，包括拓扑不变性及与多体纠缠的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全息对偶中，探索边界区域纠缠结构对应的体时空几何特征，特别是多体纠缠的几何描述。

Method: 定义EWP为边界区域A_i的纠缠楔交集的并集外部区域；在AdS3真空中利用Gauss-Bonnet定理证明其拓扑性质；在高维通过真空、黑膜、孤子及含端面膜的边界共形场论等具体例子进行分析。

Result: 在AdS3真空中EWP是拓扑量；在高维各种例子中EWP表现出非平凡几何性质；给出了混合态的推广；发现EWP可能与多体纠缠度量存在联系。

Conclusion: EWP是全息纠缠结构中一个有趣的新几何对象，具有拓扑性质，可能为多体纠缠的全息描述提供新视角。

Abstract: In this work we consider a particular codimension-1 region of a holographic spacetime which we call the entanglement wedge polygon (EWP). For a pure state and a partition of the boundary into a number of regions $A_i$ the EWP is defined as the region external to all the individual homology regions $r_{A_i}$ which consists of the intersection of the entanglement wedge EW($A_i$) with the time slice. In vacuum AdS$_3$ the quantity is topological as a direct consequence of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem. In higher dimensions we make progress by considering a number of concrete examples including vacuum, black brane, and soliton solutions of AdS$_{d+1}$ as well as spacetime geometries with end of the world branes dual to boundary conformal field theories. We provide a suitable generalization to mixed states and comment on possible connections between the EWP and measures of multi-partite entanglement.

</details>


### [107] [Phase diagram of the massless Gross-Neveu model with two components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21246)
*Michael Thies*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Gross-Neveu模型中将有限密度研究推广至有限温度，通过稳定性分析而非求解热Hartree-Fock问题，构建了包含非均匀相的完整大N相图，并发现临界填充分数处相图发生定性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Benini等人关于Gross-Neveu模型有限密度（化学势作用于部分费米子味）的精确研究推广至有限温度情形，探索温度效应对相图的影响。

Method: 采用半经典研究方法，基于各类稳定性分析（而非直接求解热Hartree-Fock问题）来确定所有相边界，从而构建完整的大N极限下相图。

Result: 成功构建了包含非均匀相在内的完整大N相图，并发现相图在某个临界填充分数处发生定性变化。

Conclusion: 通过稳定性分析可有效替代直接求解热Hartree-Fock问题来确定相边界，温度效应显著改变了有限密度下的相图结构。

Abstract: Recently, Benini, Mamroud, Reis and Serone have presented exact results for the Gross-Neveu model at finite density, both at finite N and in the large N limit. Generalizing previous studies, they introduce a chemical potential acting only on a subset of fermion flavors - the two-component Gross-Neveu model. Here, we take up this idea and extend the semiclassical study to finite temperature. The full (large N) phase diagram including inhomogeneous phases is constructed without solving the thermal Hartree-Fock problem. All phase boundaries can be found on the basis of various kinds of stability analyses. As a result, we find a qualitative change of the phase diagram at some critical filling fraction.

</details>


### [108] [Non-relativistic limits of $\mathcal N=4$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory and S-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21494)
*Hyungrok Kim,Joseph Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维最大超对称杨-米尔斯理论的非相对论极限及其与SL(2;ℤ) S-对偶性的关系，利用IIB型膜构造构建了一族非相对论极限理论，这些理论是超对称伽利略杨-米尔斯理论或BPS单极子模空间上量子力学的拓扑形变，并展示了PSL(2;ℤ)对偶性在这些理论中比在相对论母理论中更丰富复杂的运作方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索四维最大超对称杨-米尔斯理论的非相对论极限，理解这些极限理论与非微扰SL(2;ℤ) S-对偶性之间的关系，揭示对偶性在非相对论框架下的新行为。

Method: 使用IIB型膜构造，包含D3-膜和(p,q)-弦；通过路径积分建立阿贝尔情况下的对偶性；在非阿贝尔情况下匹配单粒子谱、伽利略时空对称性和电/磁可逆一形式对称性来支持论断。

Result: 得到一族拓扑形变理论：伽利略杨-米尔斯理论形变包括θ项和单极子荷耦合，模空间理论仅含θ项形变；这些理论构成具有非平凡拓扑的三维模空间，PSL(2;ℤ)对偶性作用比相对论母理论更丰富复杂。

Conclusion: 建立了非相对论极限与S-对偶性之间的深刻联系，揭示了对偶性在非相对论框架下的新结构，为理解超对称规范理论的非相对论行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate non-relativistic limits of four-dimensional maximally supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory (4d MSYM) and their relation to the nonperturbative $\operatorname{SL}(2;\mathbb Z)$ S-duality of the relativistic theory. We construct a general family of non-relativistic limits using a Type IIB brane set-up with a D3-brane and $(p,q)$-strings and show that the resulting theories are topological deformations of supersymmetric Galilean Yang-Mills theory or quantum mechanics on the moduli space of BPS monopoles. The deformations of the Galilean Yang-Mills theory are the familiar $θ$-term and a coupling to the monopole charge, while in the moduli space theory the only deformation is a $θ$-term. This family of theories fit together into a three-dimensional moduli space with nontrivial topology, on which $\operatorname{PSL}(2;\mathbb Z)$-valued dualities act in a richer and more complex way than in the relativistic parent theory. In the Abelian case, we establish the duality directly using the path integral, while in the non-Abelian case we support our claim by matching the one-particle spectrum as well as the Galilean spacetime symmetries and electric/magnetic invertible one-form symmetries.

</details>


### [109] [Asymptotically safe quantum gravity and its phenomenology -- a review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21522)
*Astrid Eichhorn*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了渐近安全量子引力（Asymptotically Safe Quantum Gravity）方法，阐述了其核心思想——量子尺度对称性，并系统总结了该领域的最新进展。研究表明，四维欧氏纯引力中已稳健确立Reuter不动点，物质场（包括标准模型及超越标准模型候选者）中量子尺度对称性证据日益充分，且洛伦兹时空签名问题近期得到解决，使得该方法在所有尺度上具有高度预测性，可应用于粒子物理、黑洞和宇宙学，为当前及近期观测检验提供了清晰路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近安全量子引力试图在量子场论框架内描述引力的量子性质，其核心动机是探寻一种自洽、可预测且能与观测对撞的量子引力理论。该综述旨在整合近年来该领域取得的重大突破，包括四维纯引力中Reuter不动点的稳健建立、物质场系统的量子尺度对称性证据，以及洛伦兹时空签名的处理，从而填补向现实量子引力描述过渡的最后空白。

Method: 该方法基于量子场论中的渐近安全假设，即理论在紫外极限下存在一个非高斯不动点（Reuter不动点），使得物理量在高能标下保持有限且可预测。研究手段包括泛函重整化群（Functional Renormalization Group）方法分析引力-物质耦合系统的重整化群流，系统考察四维欧氏及洛伦兹时空下纯引力和包含标准模型物质场的情形，并通过不动点存在性和临界指数等指标验证量子尺度对称性。

Result: 1) 四维欧氏纯引力中稳健确立了Reuter不动点，证实量子尺度对称性存在；2) 包含标准模型及超越标准模型候选者的物质场系统同样展现出量子尺度对称性；3) 成功将洛伦兹时空签名纳入框架，弥合了通向现实量子引力描述的最后差距；4) 该方法在所有能标上具有高度预测性。

Conclusion: 渐近安全量子引力已发展成为一个高度自洽且可预测的量子引力方案，在粒子物理、黑洞物理和宇宙学中均具有可观的应用前景。该综述倡导通过不同量子引力方法间的交叉比较，发现量子引力的普适物理特征，为未来理论发展和观测验证指明了方向。

Abstract: Asymptotically safe quantum gravity is an approach to quantum gravity. It is based on the premise that quantum field theory can describe the quantum nature of gravity in our universe. At its core lies quantum scale symmetry. This review provides an introduction to the key ideas of the approach and surveys the current status of the field. Over the last years, the field has taken large strides towards an increasingly realistic setting: First, compelling evidence for quantum scale symmetry exists in four-dimensional, Euclidean, pure gravity, establishing the Reuter fixed point robustly. Second, matter fields, including the Standard Model as well as beyond-Standard-Model-candidates, have been studied in depth, with increasingly conclusive evidence for quantum scale symmetry. Most recently, the final gap to a realistic description of quantum gravity is being closed, because Lorentzian spacetime signature can now be accounted for. As a consequence of quantum scale symmetry in the ultraviolet, the approach is highly predictive at all scales. This review discusses the physics of asymptotic safety across all scales. Predictive power for particle physics, black holes and cosmology provides a clear pathway to confronting quantum gravity with current and near-future observations. The review closes by discussing the connection to other approaches to quantum gravity. It advocates the perspective that such connections between approaches may lead us to an understanding of universal physical features of quantum gravity.

</details>


### [110] [Near-horizon modifications in finite $N$ holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21537)
*Rishkrith Bairy,Mrityunjay Nath,Debajyoti Sarkar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究通过在近水平修改的AdS$_2$和BTZ黑洞背景下进行HKLL边界算子重构，发现当AdS/CFT对偶字典扩展至有限大N时，体微因果性会出现非微扰违反。近水平修改由喉部参数控制，该参数设定了非局域性的尺度。在三维体维度中，对喉部参数取平均后，探针动力学谱因子呈现dip-ramp-plateau结构，这与拉伸视界或砖墙背景中的最新发现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AdS/CFT外推字典扩展至有限大N时，预期会出现非微扰的体微因果性违反，之前通过引入晚期边界时间截断并用HKLL核涂抹边界算子实现。本文动机是在近水平修改的黑洞背景中验证这一违反。

Method: 在近水平修改的AdS$_2$和BTZ黑洞背景下，利用HKLL核进行显式体重构，分析非局域性；在三维情形中，对喉部参数取平均研究谱因子结构。

Result: 恢复了与之前相同的非局域性估计；喉部参数控制修改尺度；三维探针动力学谱因子在平均后呈现dip-ramp-plateau结构。

Conclusion: 近水平修改的黑洞背景中非局域性由喉部参数调控，且谱因子结构与拉伸视界/砖墙模型一致，暗示这种非局域性结构具有普适性。

Abstract: If one extends the AdS/CFT extrapolate dictionary to large but finite $N$, we are expected to obtain non-perturbative violations of bulk micro-causality. Previously this was achieved by implementing a late boundary time cut-off, while smearing the boundary operator via the HKLL kernel. By performing explicit bulk reconstructions in the backgrounds of near-horizon modified AdS$_2$ and BTZ black holes, we recover the same non-locality estimates as above. For these black hole mimickers, the near-horizon modification is controlled by a throat parameter which sets the scale of this non-locality. In three bulk dimensions, probe dynamics also exhibits a dip-ramp-plateau structure in their spectral form factor when averaged over the throat parameter. Such structure has also been found recently in the background with a stretched horizon or a brick wall.

</details>


### [111] [Asymptotic limits of constrained instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21561)
*Benjamin Elder,Kinga Gawrych,Arttu Rajantie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We revisit the topic of false vacuum decay in field theory. We focus on a toy model of a real massive scalar field with an unstable quartic potential. This model has a false vacuum, and decay out of the false vacuum can be described via the method of constrained instantons, which work by introducing a constraint on the path integral. We identify and develop three different asymptotic limits which enable analytic construction of approximate {constrained} solutions. The first, in which the constrained solution is small compared to the inverse mass of the scalar field, is an application of the perturbative methods of Affleck, although we re-derive the main results and identify several terms which were previously neglected. Second, for very large constrained solutions we adapt the thin-wall approximation of Coleman. However, we find that the large instanton limit does not always exist. In this case we identify another useful limit, in which the Lagrange multiplier used to implement the constraint is large. In this limit, the solution's scaling with the parameters may be found via dimensional analysis and an exact solution is obtained with a single numerical computation.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum Gravity Cutoff from Axions: A Type IIB Landscape Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21650)
*Matthew Reece,Tom Rudelius,Christopher Tudball*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在IIB型弦理论Calabi-Yau紧化框架下，定量研究了超维度轴子对量子引力截断能标的约束$Λ_\mathrm{QG} \lesssim 2π\sqrt{S} f$，证明该约束在Kähler模空间边界（包括轴子弦共标关系失效的区域）仍然成立，为该约束作为量子引力一般特征提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证超维度轴子性质所导出的量子引力截断能标上限$Λ_\mathrm{QG} \lesssim 2π\sqrt{S} f$在弱耦合弦理论中的普适性，特别是在模空间边界等极端区域是否仍然成立。

Method: 在IIB型弦理论Calabi-Yau紧化背景下，分别考虑$C_2$和$C_4$轴子，通过解析分析和数值计算相结合的方式，系统研究该约束在Kähler模空间各个区域（包括边界）的表现。

Result: 该约束在Kähler模空间边界（包括轴子弦共标关系失效的区域）依然成立，为约束的普适性提供了定量证据。

Conclusion: 该结果支持了基于自然性和幺正性提出的观点，即该约束是量子引力中超维度轴子的普遍特征。

Abstract: Extra-dimensional axions have coupling strength related to fundamental, ultraviolet physics. It has been proposed that the properties of such axions imply a bound on the quantum gravity cutoff: $Λ_\mathrm{QG} \lesssim 2π\sqrt{S} f$, where $f$ is the axion decay constant and $S$ is the instanton action. In the context of weakly-coupled string theory, we identify $Λ_\mathrm{QG}$ with the string scale $M_s$. In this paper, we carry out a quantitative study of this bound on the string scale in the context of Calabi-Yau compactifications of Type IIB string theory, considering both $C_2$ and $C_4$ axions. We show, both analytically and numerically, that the bound holds even near boundaries of the Kähler moduli space, including those where the co-scaling relationship for axion strings fails. This evidence bolsters previous arguments, based on naturalness and on unitarity, that the bound is a general feature of extra-dimensional axions in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [113] [On the Universality of Probe Complexity in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21662)
*Eric L. Graef,Jeff Murugan,Horatiu Nastase,Hendrik J. R. Van Zyl*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了平面$\mathcal{N}=4$超Yang-Mills理论中，对偶于粘在巨引力子上的开弦的单迹算子的Krylov复杂度。在受保护少体扇区中，Krylov动力学由种子谱测度对应的正交多项式理论支配，导致Lanczos系数仅由谱支撑决定，且呈带限可积行为。作者提出有限密度方案，其中磁子数随系统尺度缩放，并建立了一个具体的普适性检验标准：领先Krylov增长是否仅依赖于粗粒热力学数据$(\rho,\varepsilon)$而非微观探针结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证近期提出的引力侧算子复杂度增长普适性猜想是否在边界场论中成立，特别是在开弦-巨引力子系统中。

Method: 利用正交多项式理论分析Krylov复杂度，推导保护少体扇区中的Lanczos系数解析形式；提出有限密度方案，将磁子数随系统尺度缩放来测试普适性。

Result: 在固定磁子数$M$和开弦长度$L\rightarrow\infty$时，得到$a_n=2Mg$和$b_n\rightarrow Mg$，表明动力学是带限可积的；发现少体扇区不足以测试引力侧普适性猜想。

Conclusion: 提出了一个精确的边界场论框架，通过有限密度方案和粗粒热力学数据来检验引力普适性猜想，为全息对偶中的复杂度增长研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate Krylov complexity for single-trace operators dual to open strings attached to giant gravitons in planar $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory. We show that in protected and few-body sectors, Krylov dynamics is governed by orthogonal polynomial theory associated to the seed spectral measure, leading to bounded Lanczos coefficients determined solely by spectral support. In particular, for fixed magnon number $M$ and open-string length $L\rightarrow\infty$, we derive $a_n=2Mg$ and $b_n\rightarrow Mg$, demonstrating integrable, band-limited dynamics. This establishes that such sectors are insufficient to test recently proposed gravity-side universality of operator complexity growth. We therefore formulate a finite-density program in which magnons scale with system size, and propose a concrete universality test: whether the leading Krylov growth depends only on coarse thermodynamic data $(ρ,\varepsilon)$ and not on microscopic probe structure. This provides a precise boundary-field-theory framework for testing gravitational universality conjectures.

</details>


### [114] [Holonomies and Boundary Symmetries in the Discrete Warped Chern-Simons Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22028)
*H. T. Özer,Aytül Filiz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维翘曲引力的离散翘曲Chern-Simons描述，基于边界完整性和单值性扇区构建离散边界框架，证明了翘曲引力热力学可以从边界单值性数据的角度理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索翘曲AdS(3)全息对偶中离散边界框架的构建，建立边界单值性与连续Wilson环之间的直接对应关系，从而从完整性的角度理解翘曲引力热力学。

Method: 基于SL(2,R)+U(1)规范结构，用有序链接完整性乘积替代连续规范联络；通过离散SL(2,R)单值性的共轭分类得到双曲、椭圆和抛物线扇区；利用单值性不变量推导离散熵关系。

Result: 推导出完全基于边界完整性数据的离散熵关系，在连续极限下重现翘曲黑洞和WCFT结构；证明连续翘曲完整性条件可由有序边界乘积的大晶格极限恢复。

Conclusion: 翘曲引力热力学可以从根本上的完整性视角理解，边界单值性扇区为离散翘曲框架中的物理态提供了另一种组织性描述方式。

Abstract: We investigate a discrete warped Chern-Simons description of three-dimensional warped gravity based on boundary holonomies and monodromy sectors. Starting from the lower-spin SL(2, R) + U(1) gauge structure associated with warped AdS(3) holography and warped conformal field theories (WCFTs), we construct a discrete boundary framework in which ordered products of link holonomies replace continuous gauge connections along noncontractible cycles. In this setting, boundary monodromies become the primary gauge-invariant observables characterizing the physical sectors of the theory. We show that the hyperbolic, eliliptic, and parabolic sectors naturally arise from the conjugacy classes of the discrete SL(2, R) monodromy, while the additional U(1) holonomy supplies the warped contribution to the boundary charges. Using these monodromy invariants, we derive a discrete entropy relation entirely from boundary holonomy data without relying on a smooth geometric thermal background. The resulting entropy reproduces the characteristic warped black-hole and WCFT structure in the continuum limit. We further demonstrate that the continuum warped holonomy conditions are recovered from the large-lattice limit of the ordered boundary products, establishing a direct correspondence between discrete monodromies and continuous Wilson loops. Our analysis suggests that warped gravitational thermodynamics may be understood from a fundamentally holonomy-based perspective in which boundary monodromy sectors provide an alternative organizational description of the physical states within the discrete warped framework.
  Keywords: warped Chern-Simons theory, boundary monodromies, holonomy sectors, Wilson loops, warped thermodynamics.

</details>


### [115] [Hamiltonian formulation of Carrollian Maxwell theory in Deformed Light-cone Kaluza-Klein-like Null reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22050)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Bargmann变形光锥背景下的类卡鲁扎-克莱因零缩减方法，从复Maxwell场构造出磁性和电性Carrollian Maxwell理论，在Carrollian极限下保持U(1)高斯约束和规范不变性，并发现了之前方法未得到的标量场耦合/退耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法构建Carrollian规范理论时未能完全保持规范不变性，且未充分揭示Carrollian理论的多样性。本文旨在发展一种能保持规范对称性的系统化构造方法。

Method: 在Bargmann变形光锥背景下，对复Maxwell场实施类卡鲁扎-克莱因零缩减，通过选择不同的标度得到磁性和电性Carrollian Maxwell理论，并在Hamiltonian形式中保持一阶U(1)高斯约束的完整性。

Result: 成功构造了磁性和电性Carrollian Maxwell理论，规范不变性在Carrollian极限下得以保持。发现标量场可以耦合或退耦方式出现在Carrollian理论中，这是先前方法未发现的。

Conclusion: 变形光锥类卡鲁扎-克莱因零缩减方法能正确应用于规范理论，揭示了Carrollian理论的多样性，拓宽了该方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We construct magnetic and electric Carrollian Maxwell theories by performing Kaluza-Klein-like null reduction of a complex Maxwell field in a Bargmann deformed light-cone background with manifest gauge symmetry. The procedure preserves a first-class U(1) Gauss constraint throughout the Carrollian limit. Gauge invariance is therefore maintained in our Hamiltonian formulation. By choosing different scalings, we obtain standard magnetic Carrollian theory and electric Carrollian theory. However, a scalar field could appear in the Carrollian theory in a coupled or decoupled way, which has not been found by previous methods. This result fully reveals the diversity of Carrollian theories accessible through the deformed light-cone Kaluza-Klein-like null reduction method. Furthermore, our work provides an explicit example of the correct application of this approach, thereby broadening the scope of its applicability to gauge theories.

</details>


### [116] [Metamorphosis of fractional instantons on a twisted $T^4$ with a double-trace deformation: a numerical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22078)
*Benjamin Dobozy,Erich Poppitz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过数值最小化格子作用量，研究了在 $T^4$ 上具有扭曲边界条件的迹变形Yang-Mills理论中分数拓扑荷的经典最小作用量位形，揭示了单极-瞬子（$k=1$）、中心涡旋（$k=2$）和分数瞬子（$k=3,4$）之间的形态转变，并指出在纯Yang-Mills理论中，即使没有变形势，这种转变依然成立，同时发现在非零变形势下某些转变可能是不连续的并涉及能级交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索迹变形Yang-Mills理论中不同几何（$R^{4-k} \times T^k$）下分数拓扑荷的最小作用量鞍点构型（单极-瞬子、中心涡旋、分数瞬子）之间的相互转变机制，以及变形势对转变的影响。

Method: 在 $T^4$ 上采用扭曲边界条件，通过数值最小化迹变形Yang-Mills理论的格点作用量，改变扭曲参数和环面周期比以插值不同几何，从而观察各类拓扑荷构型的形态演变。

Result: （1）通过改变几何参数，观察到了单极-瞬子、中心涡旋和分数瞬子之间的连续形态转变；（2）论证了即便没有变形势，在特定环面形状下（变形导致的阿贝尔化与扭曲导致的阿贝尔化一致），纯Yang-Mills理论中也存在类似的通量准直（单极-瞬子链汇聚成中心涡旋面）的物理图像；（3）非零变形势下，某些最小作用量分数荷构型之间的转变可能是不连续的，涉及能级交叉。

Conclusion: 迹变形Yang-Mills理论中分数拓扑荷构型之间的转变依赖于几何参数和变形势；纯Yang-Mills理论在特定条件下也能呈现类似的鞍点构型转变；变形势的存在可能导致一级相变式的能级交叉行为。

Abstract: We use numerical minimization of the lattice action of trace-deformed Yang-Mills theory on $T^4$ with twisted boundary conditions to find the classical minimum action configurations of fractional topological charge. We vary the twists and ratios of torus periods to interpolate between different $R^{4-k} \times T^k$ geometries. This allows us to see how the corresponding minimum action saddle point configurations -- monopole-instantons ($k=1$), center vortices ($k=2$), and fractional instantons ($k=3,4$) -- morph into each other. We also study how the transition between them depends on the presence of a deformation potential. In particular, we argue that the recent analytic picture of chains of monopole-instantons collimating their flux into center-vortex sheets, while technically relying on the deformation potential, also holds in pure Yang-Mills theory, for tori whose shape causes the abelianization due to the deformation to align with the one due to the twists. Our results also indicate that with nonzero deformation potential, some transitions between different minimal-action fractional charge configurations may be discontinuous and involve level crossing.

</details>


### [117] [Thermodynamic and Topological Phase Transitions of AdS Black Holes with Nonminimal $F^{αβ}F^{γλ}R_{αγ}R_{βλ}$ Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22204)
*Mehdi Sadeghi,Faramarz Rahmani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了含非最小耦合项F^{αβ}F^{γλ}R_{αγ}R_{βλ}的四维渐近AdS黑洞的常规相变和拓扑相变。在一阶微扰下推导了热力学量，验证了热力学第一定律和Smarr关系。ε=0时为标准Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞，属于W^{1+}拓扑类(W=+1)；引入非最小耦合后拓扑转变为W^{0-}类(W=0)。在该拓扑转变过程中，van der Waals型一级相变在中温区仍然存在，并嵌入整体Hawking-Page模式中，形成混合型黑洞热力学系统。耦合参数ε充当拓扑形变参数，尽管解是微扰性的，仍能改变系统的普适分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非最小耦合项F^{αβ}F^{γλ}R_{αγ}R_{βλ}对AdS黑洞的常规相变行为及拓扑相变的影响，特别是验证该耦合能否改变黑洞热力学系统的拓扑分类。

Method: 采用一阶微扰方法处理非最小耦合项，推导热力学量，验证热力学第一定律和Smarr关系，并通过拓扑分类分析相变特征。

Result: 耦合参数ε充当拓扑形变参数，使黑洞的拓扑分类从W^{1+}(W=+1)转变为W^{0-}(W=0)，同时van der Waals型一级相变在中间区域仍保留，嵌入Hawking-Page模式中，形成混合热力学系统。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合项能够从根本上改变黑洞热力学系统的拓扑分类，且这种改变发生在微扰框架下，表明拓扑分类对比耦合强度不敏感。

Abstract: The conventional and topological phase transitions of a four-dimensional asymptotically AdS black hole with a non-minimal coupling term $F^{αβ}F^{γλ}R_{αγ}R_{βλ}$ are investigated. Using a perturbative approach to first order in the coupling $ε$, the thermodynamic quantities are derived and the first law and Smarr relation are verified. Intriguingly, while the $ε= 0$ limit yields the standard Reissner--Nordström--AdS black hole belonging to the $W^{1+}$ topological class ($W = +1$), switching on the non-minimal coupling fundamentally transforms the topology to the $W^{0-}$ class ($W = 0$). This transition occurs while the van der Waals-type first-order phase transition survives in the intermediate region, embedded within the overall Hawking--Page pattern, rendering the system a hybrid black hole thermodynamic system. The coupling $ε$ thus acts as a topological deformation parameter that alters the universal classification of the system, despite the perturbative nature of the solution.

</details>


### [118] [Hilbert Functions and Line Bundle Cohomology on CICY Threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22275)
*Juntao Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Koszul谱序列中的Hilbert函数系统解释了完全交Calabi-Yau三维流形上线丛上同调的分片多项式结构，将经验公式转化为秩亏缺陈述，并提供了解析证明或有限框验证，同时发现了新的墙结构并构建了新的上同调公式库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线丛上同调是弦现象学中的重要输入，此前基于外推和机器学习的方法仅发现其在Picard格上具有分片多项式描述，但缺乏理论解释。作者希望从数学上阐明这一结构的内在原因。

Method: 利用Koszul谱序列中非平凡映射的Hilbert函数，结合Bott-Borel-Weil理论确定非零环境上同调群，将上同调维数问题转化为Koszul映射的秩亏缺问题，通过余核或核模的Hilbert函数进行刻画。

Result: 解析地恢复了文献中几乎所有分片公式；对剩余情形证明了有限框验证定理；发现了某些CICY上新的墙结构，细化了有限数据范围给出的室分解；为两个此前未分析的CICY三维流形构建了线丛上同调公式库。

Conclusion: Hilbert函数方法能有效解释并扩展线丛上同调的公式化描述，为未来基于线丛构建的弦模型扫描提供更快速的输入。

Abstract: Line bundle cohomology on complete intersection Calabi--Yau threefolds is an important input in string phenomenology. Previous work, based on direct extrapolation and on machine-learning analyses of finite data sets, has shown that these cohomology dimensions often admit chamber-wise polynomial descriptions on the Picard lattice. In this paper we explain this structure through the Hilbert functions associated with the non-trivial maps in the Koszul spectral sequence. Once Bott--Borel--Weil theory determines the non-vanishing ambient cohomology groups, the rank defects of the relevant Koszul maps are governed by Hilbert functions of cokernel or kernel modules. This turns many empirical chamber formulae into explicit rank-defect statements, with proofs that are either analytic or certified on finite boxes. Using this approach, we recover analytically almost all of the piecewise formulae appearing in the existing literature. For the remaining cases, we prove finite-box certificate theorems for infinite families of line bundles in the specified regions. We also identify new wall structures on certain CICYs which refine the chamber decompositions suggested by finite-range data. Finally, we use the same framework to construct line bundle cohomology formula libraries for two CICY threefolds which had not previously been analysed in this way. These results suggest that Hilbert-function methods can provide promising faster inputs for future string model-building scans based on line bundle constructions.

</details>


### [119] [Eight loop form factors, amplitudes and patterns in planar $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22380)
*Lance J. Dixon,Zhenjie Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论中${\rm tr}φ^3$算子的三点形状因子至8圈，并发现了与${\rm tr}φ^2$形状因子相同的多重对数函数空间，以及符号系数序列的全阶模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平面$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论中，六胶子最大螺旋度违背振幅已通过对跖对偶性计算至8圈，该对偶将其联系到受保护算子（手征应力张量超多重态，即${\rm tr}φ^2$）的三点形状因子。本文旨在将类似计算推广到另一算子${\rm tr}φ^3$的三点形状因子，探索其结构与全阶模式。

Method: 利用对跖对偶性以及多重对数函数空间的结构限制，计算${\rm tr}φ^3$算子的三点形状因子至8圈，分析其符号（symbol）中系数序列的模式。

Result: ${\rm tr}φ^3$形状因子与${\rm tr}φ^2$形状因子属于相同的受限制的多重对数函数空间；给出了${\rm tr}φ^3$形状因子主导不连续性的符号系数序列的全阶模式。

Conclusion: ${\rm tr}φ^3$三点形状因子与${\rm tr}φ^2$三点形状因子共享相同的函数空间，且其符号系数展示出可归纳的全阶规律，有助于深入理解平面$\mathcal{N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论中的散射振幅与形状因子结构。

Abstract: The simplest nontrivial amplitude in planar $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory is six-gluon scattering in the maximally-helicity-violating configuration. It has been computed to 8 loops with the help of antipodal duality, which relates it to the three-point form factor of a protected operator, the chiral stress tensor super-multiplet, represented also as ${\rm tr} φ^2$. In this talk, we describe the computation to 8 loops of another three-point form factor, for the operator ${\rm tr}φ^3$. This form factor lives in the same restricted space of polylogarithms as the ${\rm tr}φ^2$ form factor. We also report on all-order patterns for sequences of coefficients in the symbols of these polylogarithmic results, for the leading discontinuity of the ${\rm tr}φ^3$ form factor.

</details>


### [120] [Quiver Approach to Symmetry Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30354)
*Vivek Chakrabhavi,Mirjam Cvetič,Jonathan J. Heckman,Shani Meynet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于探针膜路径代数的代数方法，用于从M理论背景的Calabi-Yau锥形中提取5D超共形场论的整体对称性异常，替代了传统的几何计算方法（如三重交点数或η不变量），并在多个环面三维流形例子中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的通过几何计算（三重交点数、η不变量）提取5D SCFT整体对称性异常的方法在某些情形下未知或组合复杂难以处理，因此需要一种互补的代数方法。

Method: 从M理论背景的Calabi-Yau锥形X出发，利用探测X的膜路径代数（path algebra of branes）来提取对称性异常数据，以此替代传统的基于解消几何的三重交点数或边界几何∂X的η不变量计算。

Result: 该方法在多个环面三维流形例子中成功应用，包括轨形ℂ³/Γ以及更一般的非轨形Sasaki-Einstein五维流形的Calabi-Yau锥形。

Conclusion: 提供了一种有效的代数替代方案，适用于传统几何计算未知或组合复杂的情形，拓展了5D SCFT对称性异常研究的工具集。

Abstract: Global symmetry anomalies of a quantum field theory (QFT) can be packaged as specific couplings of a higher-dimensional symmetry theory (SymTh). In this work we show that for 5D superconformal field theories (SCFTs) engineered from M-theory backgrounds $X$ a Calabi-Yau cone, this data can be extracted from the path algebra of branes probing $X$. This provides a complementary algebraic approach compared with more geometric computations based on the explicit calculation of triple intersection numbers in a resolved geometry and / or $η$-invariants extracted from the boundary geometry $\partial X$. Our method applies in situations where the counterpart geometric computation is either unknown or combinatorially unwieldy. We illustrate with several toric threefold examples, including orbifolds $\mathbb{C}^{3} / Γ$ and more general non-orbifold Calabi-Yau cones of Sasaki-Einstein five-manifolds.

</details>


### [121] [Holographic s+p superconductors with nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22459)
*Ru-Qing Chen,Hui Zeng,Zhang-Yu Nie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息s+p超导模型中引入非线性电动力学，发现非线性参数b的增大会抑制s波和p波凝聚，且对s波抑制更强，导致s+p共存相需要更小的电荷比。特别地，电磁场的非线性自相互作用导致即使在无凝聚时电荷也在视界外自发积累，使得传统超导电荷密度公式失效。作者提出了修正定义，并发现该积累电荷会改变正常相的光学电导率，使其虚部在有限频率出现极小值，这干扰了超导能隙频率的提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性电动力学效应对全息s+p超导模型的影响，特别是非线性参数如何改变不同超导相（纯s波、纯p波、s+p共存相）的竞争关系，以及非线性自相互作用带来的新物理现象。

Method: 在探针极限下构建耦合非线性电动力学的全息s+p超导模型，数值求解运动方程，通过计算凝聚值、巨势、相图以及光学电导率来系统分析非线性参数b的影响。

Result: 1) 非线性参数b增大同时抑制s波和p波凝聚，且对s波抑制更强；2) b-T相图中纯s波相区域随b增大而收缩，s+p共存相需要的电荷比q_p/q_s更小；3) 电磁场非线性自相互作用导致电荷在事件视界外自发积累，即使在正常相也存在；4) 正常相光学电导率因积累电荷而改变，虚部在有限频率出现极小值，该极小值在超导相中靠近临界点处仍存在，干扰能隙频率提取。

Conclusion: 非线性电动力学显著影响全息超导的相结构和电磁响应，需要重新定义超导电荷密度，且非线性效应可能遮蔽超导能隙的读取，对实验分析有重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate a holographic s+p superconductor model coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics in the probe limit. The equations of motion are solved numerically, and the condensates as well as the grand potential curves for phase transitions at different values of the nonlinear parameter $b$ are illustrated. It is found that as $b$ increases, both the pure s-wave and p-wave condensates are suppressed. From the $b-T$ phase diagram, we observe that the region of the pure s-wave phase gradually shrinks with increasing $b$, which is attributed to the stronger suppression on the s-wave condensate compared to the one on the p-wave. Moreover, a smaller charge ratio $q_p/q_s$ is needed for the s+p coexistent phase to appear as $b$ grows. A particularly interesting feature is that, due to the nonlinear self-interaction of the electromagnetic field, charge accumulates spontaneously outside the event horizon from the bulk perspective even in the absence of scalar and vector condensates, thereby invalidating the conventional formula for the superconducting charge density. We improve the definition of the superconducting charge density as the background-subtracted value of the accumulated charge outside the horizon with respect to that in the normal phase. Furthermore, the optical conductivity in the normal phase is also modified by this accumulated charge for finite $b$, and its imaginary part develops a minimum at a finite frequency. This minimum persists in the superconducting phase near the critical point, confusing the extraction of the gap frequency.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清近期文献中讨论的带规范伸缩对称性的无张力弦与更早的共形弦理论之间的关系，验证其是否为共形弦约化到闵可夫斯基时空的特殊情形。

Result: 证实了带规范伸缩对称性的无张力弦是共形弦约化到闵可夫斯基时空的结果；揭示了Virasoro代数与su(1,1) Kac-Moody代数的半直积在d维中映射为Corrollian-Weyl对称性。

Conclusion: 通过共形空间的Dirac约化，建立了无张力弦理论与共形弦理论之间的明确联系，并阐明了相关对称性代数的约化对应关系。

Abstract: In \cite{Lindstrom:2026quz} it is pointed out how the tensionless string with a gauged scale symmetry discussed in the recent articles, \cite{Sheikh-Jabbari:2026cnj,Sheikh-Jabbari:2026vqh,Sheikh-Jabbari:2026tpf}, is a reduction of the conformal string \cite{Gustafsson:1994kr} to Minkowski space. Here we corroborate this by choices of slices in Dirac $d+2$ dimensional conformal space. We perform a Dirac reduction of the model and its algebra of constraints and see how they map to the constraints in $d$ dimensions, including how the semidirect product of the Virasoro algebra with a su(1,1) Kac-Moody algebra becomes the Corrollian-Weyl symmetry in $d$ dimensions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用校准于码通道的恢复映射并沿逻辑态族固定；推导精确有限分辨率判据；在双曲盘附近导出正则原面积二射流来自度规二射流的充要条件；将有限网络转化为多面体实现问题；引入测地线X射线变换刻画几何切空间；通过度量强迫的Jacobi方程确定重正化边界长度像的Hessian并得到规范不变二次障碍。

Result: 得到了正则原面积二射流可来自度规二射流的充要条件；建立了有限网络的对偶认证和稳定重构机制；给出了显式的非几何性见证；揭示了哈密顿偏斜码实现一阶非几何性和二阶阻挫的响应。

Conclusion: 兼容的度规扰动可模边界固定微分同胚重构；二射流判据对正则数据是无条件的；在分裂正则性假设下，近邻几何数据形成局部图。

Abstract: State-dependent proto-area data produced by approximate recovery need not be compatible with a single local bulk metric. Using recovery maps calibrated on the code channel and held fixed along a logical-state family, we derive exact finite-resolution criteria and, near the hyperbolic disk, necessary and sufficient conditions for a regular proto-area two-jet to arise from a metric two-jet on a time-reflection-symmetric asymptotically AdS$_3$ slice. Finite networks give a polyhedral realization problem with primal and dual certificates, stable reconstruction, and explicit witnesses of nongeometry. In the continuum, the geometric tangent space is the range of the rank-two geodesic X-ray transform. A metric-forced Jacobi equation determines the normal Hessian of the renormalized boundary-length image and yields a gauge-invariant quadratic obstruction. Under a split-regularity hypothesis, nearby geometric data form a local graph; the two-jet criterion itself is unconditional for regular data. Hamiltonian-skewed codes realize both first-order nongeometry and a response whose first obstruction appears only at quadratic order. The compatible metric perturbation is reconstructed modulo boundary-fixing diffeomorphisms.

</details>


### [124] [Exact solution of the Seven-Vertex Model on a dynamical lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22651)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对文献[1]提出的动态格点上六顶点模型的单参数变形（即引力七顶点模型）给出完整解析解，填补此前仅得到标度极限解的空白。

Method: 将统计模型映射为动态三角剖分上的自避和互避环气体，通过大N矩阵模型（7vMM）重新表述，利用雅可比θ函数给出谱曲线的参数形式，并结合宇宙常数与温度两个耦合常数分析相图。

Result: 得到了7vMM以雅可比θ函数表示的完整非代数谱曲线；在耦合常数空间中绘制了相图，识别出大质量相、稀相和密相三个临界相；在分离密相与大质量环的三重临界点附近，恢复了文献[1]的标度解作为全解的新近形式。

Conclusion: 引力七顶点模型与引力O(n)环模型具有相似的相结构（大质量、稀相、密相），但由于环权重依赖于环的形状和格点曲率缺陷（通过格点自旋连接），二者存在重要差异。该模型通过大N矩阵方法实现了精确可解性。

Abstract: We give the complete solution of the one-parameter deformation of the six-vertex model on dynamical lattice introduced in [1] and dubbed gravitational seven-vertex model. The statistical model in question is mapped to a gas of self- and mutually avoiding loops on dynamical triangulations, with a temperature coupling controlling the volume not occupied by loops. The phase diagram is characterised by massive, dilute and dense critical phases, similarly to the gravitational O(n) loop model. There is however an important difference -- in our model the weights of the loops are not topological but depend on the form of the loop and on the curvature defects of the lattice via lattice spin connection. The seven-vertex model on dynamical lattice is nevertheless exactly solvable after being reformulated as a large-N matrix model, which we will refer to as 7vMM, and the solution in the scaling limit was found in [1]. Here we derive the full solution in terms of Jacobi theta functions and present the (non-algebraic) spectral curve of 7vMM in a parametric form. We obtain the phase diagram in the space of the two coupling constants -- the cosmological constant and the temperature -- and identify the critical phases along the boundary of the physical domain. We derive the scaling solution of [1] as the asymptotic of the full solution in the vicinity of the tricritical point separating the phases of dense and massive loops.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期发现无限温SYK模型的准正则模呈圣诞树形状，与AdS黑洞类似，本文将其推广到有限温度，以连续连接无限温结果与对偶于JT引力的低温区域。

Result: 发现仅强耦合（引力区域）下弛豫率随温度单调增加；获得了关于算子增长的独立新结果。

Abstract: It was recently found that the quasinormal modes (or Ruelle--Pollicott resonances) of the SYK model at infinite temperature form a Christmas tree shape, reminiscent of AdS black holes. We generalise this computation to finite temperature, allowing us to continuously connect the infinite temperature results to the low temperature regime dual to JT gravity. We contrast the movement of the quasinormal modes with a few examples: various AdS black holes, dynamical phase transitions, and the large $p$ SYK chain. We find that the relaxation rate increases monotonically with temperature only at strong coupling, corresponding to the gravitational regime. Byproducts of our investigations are new results on operator growth that may be of independent interest.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出振荡子（oscillon）可理解为局域离散共振（非归一化）模式。真空中的振荡子源于阈值模，因非线性作用而局域化。基于此发现扭结的非线性激发——摇摆子（wobblerons），即振荡子-扭结束缚态。振荡子也可起源于反束缚模，即离散但非归一化的正能模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示振荡子作为局域离散共振模的本质，并拓展到扭结系统中的非线性激发（摇摆子）以及反束缚模起源的振荡子。

Method: 理论分析：将振荡子解释为局域离散共振（非归一化）模式，分别从阈值模（因非线性局域化）和反束缚模两种途径出发，并应用于扭结系统发现振荡子-扭结束缚态。

Result: 真空中的振荡子可由阈值模经非线性局域化产生；发现了摇摆子——扭结的非线性激发，即振荡子-扭结束缚态；振荡子也可起源于反束缚模（离散正能非归一化模式）。

Conclusion: 振荡子可以统一理解为局域离散共振模式，其起源包括阈值模和反束缚模两种途径，并且振荡子可与扭结形成束缚态（摇摆子）。

Abstract: We show that an oscillon can be understood as a localized discrete resonant (non-normalizable) mode. Specifically, oscillon in the vacuum arises from the threshold mode, which because of nonlinearity gets localized. Following this idea, we find {\it wobblerons} - nonlinear excitations of kinks, that is, oscillons-kink bound state. Now, the oscillon can also originate in an antibound mode, i.e., a discrete, positive energy but non-normalizable mode.

</details>


### [127] [UV-finite Effective Field Theory from Quantized Irreversible Null-geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22788)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微扰量子场论长期受到紫外发散和重叠子发散问题的困扰，需要复杂重整化方案来处理，本研究旨在从根本上建立一个内在自洽且绝对有限的场论框架。

Method: 在四维时空本征地嵌入双指数容量测度（源于量子化不可逆零几何）；通过精确算符解解析展开，证明宏观固有时界T≥τ₀自然涌现；将BPHZ协议严格作为代数映射算子使用；生成解析封闭的非微扰Schwinger-Dyson方程。

Result: QED中电子裸质量m₀≈0.427 MeV，精确保持Ward-Takahashi恒等式Z₁=Z₂；QCD中可计算有限裸强耦合α_{s,0}≈0.0191，精确保持Slavnov-Taylor恒等式。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限有效场论提供了自洽、无反常的数学基础，可进一步处理动态手征对称性破缺和质量生成问题。

Abstract: Standard perturbative quantum field theory is persistently challenged by ultraviolet (UV) divergences and overlapping sub-divergences. In this work, we construct an absolutely UV-finite effective field theory (EFT) natively in four-dimensional spacetime. We embed the complete dynamical operator within a double-exponential capacity measure derived from quantized irreversible null-geometry. By analytically expanding the exact operator resolvent, we demonstrate that a universal macroscopic proper-time bound ($T \ge τ_0$) natively emerges, structurally collapsing the high-order UV phase space via topological contraction. This mathematically defines the absolute UV boundary of the EFT. Consequently, overlapping subgraph divergences are rigorously identified as distributional pseudo-singularities caused by the continuous approximation analytically oversmoothing the discrete lattice bounds. These artifacts are systematically corrected by employing the Bogoliubov-Parasiuk-Hepp-Zimmermann (BPHZ) protocol strictly as an algebraic mapping operator. Evaluating Quantum Electrodynamics in 4D, we obtain a finite electron bare mass ($m_0 \approx 0.427$ MeV) and derive finite renormalization constants, analytically preserving the exact Ward-Takahashi identity ($Z_1=Z_2$). In Quantum Chromodynamics, the exact preservation of Slavnov-Taylor identities yields a calculable finite bare strong coupling ($α_{s,0} \approx 0.0191$). Generating analytically closed non-perturbative Schwinger-Dyson Equations, this framework provides a consistent, anomaly-free mathematical foundation for finite effective field theories to address dynamic chiral symmetry breaking and mass generation.

</details>


### [128] [A Linearized Obstruction to the Supersymmetric Extension of Conformal Boundary Conditions in Euclidean Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22810)
*Xingyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明在微扰极小超引力中，Witten共形边界条件无法扩展为半超对称边界条件。对于固定共形玻色子数据，任何手征性引力子边界条件都会破坏超对称性，障碍源于最高导数阶的线性化问题，但非线性超协变边界项可能绕过此障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Witten共形边界条件（固定边界共形类与平均曲率，留下无迹外曲率作为共轭响应）在微扰极小超引力中能否扩展为半超对称边界条件。

Method: 在微扰极小超引力框架下，检验多种引力子边界条件（包括局域、伪微分、APS型等）与固定共形玻色子数据的兼容性，分析其能否保持手征超对称性。

Result: 任何半维引力子边界条件都无法在固定共形玻色子数据下闭合完整的手征超对称性。超对称先选择自然局域手征引力子数据，该数据作用后产生无迹外曲率——这正是共形处方未固定的响应。障碍是切向动量点态且出现在最高导数阶，不能被规范变换补偿。

Conclusion: 障碍是线性化、最高导数阶的，而非全局或非线性不可行；非线性超协变边界项可能通过将无迹外曲率与引力子双线性耦合来规避此问题。

Abstract: Witten's conformal boundary condition \cite{Witten:2018lgb} provides an elliptic boundary-value problem for the finite-boundary perturbative Euclidean gravitational path integral: one fixes the boundary conformal class and the mean curvature, while the trace-free extrinsic curvature is left free as the conjugate response. We show that this perturbative construction admits no half-supersymmetric extension in linearized minimal supergravity. For fixed conformal bosonic data, no half-dimensional gravitino boundary condition (local or pseudodifferential, APS-type included, with any compatible ghost condition at highest-derivative order) closes the full preserved chiral supersymmetry. Supersymmetry first selects the natural local chiral gravitino datum. Acting back on this datum then produces the trace-free extrinsic curvature, precisely the response that the conformal prescription leaves unfixed. The obstruction is therefore not the failure of a particular elliptic ansatz: even the chiral/Robin completion that is LS-elliptic and BRST-compatible at highest-derivative order would impose Dirichlet control on a Neumann response. The obstruction is pointwise in tangential momentum and survives compensating gauge transformations. It is a linearized, highest-derivative obstruction, not a global or nonlinear no-go; nonlinear supercovariant boundary terms may evade it by tying the trace-free extrinsic curvature to gravitino bilinears.

</details>


### [129] [Extended Thermodynamics and Renyi Entropy Beyond Fixed Central Charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22849)
*Chatchai Promsiri,Phuwadon Chunaksorn,Ratchaphat Nakarachinda,Ekapong Hirunsirisawat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Casini-Huerta-Myers (CHM)映射，在固定电荷、场论体积和中心荷势的巨正则系综中构造了一种新型的中心荷Rényi熵，证明其满足所有Rényi熵基本不等式，并揭示了近极端构型中中心荷扇区产生的残余熵，以及由质量隙标度定义的特征Rényi指数n*所区分的两种统计区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形热力学框架中，中心荷C的变分解释尚待解决；本文旨在通过构造一种新型Rényi熵来捕捉具有涨落自由度的全息CFT系综中的纠缠程度。

Method: 基于CHM映射，在固定(Q̃,V,μ_C)巨正则系综中构造中心荷Rényi熵；通过共形热力学分析近极端构型，研究质量隙和特征温度标度T̃*向特征Rényi指数n*的映射。

Result: 该熵在μ_C的允许范围内满足所有四条Rényi熵基本不等式；近极端构型中残余熵源于中心荷扇区而非热激发；由质量隙定义了特征Rényi指数n*，区分了主导理论区域(n>n*)和多理论区域(n<n*)。

Conclusion: 该新型熵是真实的Rényi度量，n*将理论空间划分为两种统计性质不同的区域：高n时由最概然CFT实现主导，低n时涨落理论和更高能模激发变得显著。

Abstract: An outstanding problem in the framework of conformal thermodynamics concerns the interpretation of variations in the central charge $C$. In this paper, we construct a novel central-charge Rényi entropy via the Casini-Huerta-Myers (CHM) map by considering thermal CFTs on a hyperbolic cylinder within a fixed charge, field theory volume and central charge potential $(\tilde{Q},\mathcal{V},μ_C)$ grand canonical ensemble. We demonstrate that the resulting entropy satisfies all four fundamental Rényi entropy inequalities throughout the admissible range of $μ_C$, establishing its consistency as a genuine Rényi measure. Physically, this novel measure extends conventional Rényi entropy by capturing the degree of entanglement across a statistical ensemble of holographic CFTs with fluctuating degrees of freedom. Furthermore, our conformal thermodynamic analysis of near-extremal configurations reveals that residual entropy arises from the central charge sector rather than thermal excitations. The mass gap that separates the extremal state and the first thermal excitation introduces a characteristic temperature scale $\tilde{T}_*$, which translates via the CHM map into a distinguished characteristic Rényi index $n_*$. Crucially, we propose that $n_*$ separates the theory space into two qualitatively distinct statistical regimes: a dominant-theory regime ($n > n_*$) governed by the most probable CFT realizations, and a multi-theory regime ($n < n_*$) where a broader spectrum of fluctuating theories and higher-energy modular excitations becomes increasingly relevant.

</details>


### [130] [On background fields and a cutoff in sigma models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23088)
*N. V. Kharuk*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以二维Heisenberg群非线性sigma模型为例，比较了两种手征场分解为背景场和涨落的方法，发现仅有一种方法与背景场生成泛函的构造一致。研究还进行了单圈量子作用量重整化、双圈近似下的幂律奇异性计算，并考虑了向扩展经典作用的过渡，最后分析了截断与背景场方法中特殊泛函关系的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较两种不同的手征场分解方案（背景场加涨落），识别出与背景场方法生成泛函构造一致的正确分解方式。

Method: 以二维Heisenberg群非线性sigma模型为实例，对比两种手征场分解方案；通过单圈重整化、双圈近似幂律奇异性计算以及扩展经典作用量分析，验证分解方法与背景场方法的一致性。

Result: 仅有一种分解方案与背景场方法的生成泛函构造一致；完成了单圈量子作用量重整化；在双圈近似下计算了幂律奇异性；实现了向扩展经典作用量的过渡；验证了截断与背景场方法中特殊泛函关系的自洽性。

Conclusion: 在二维Heisenberg群非线性sigma模型中，正确的背景场分解方案是确保背景场方法自洽性的关键，后续的重整化计算和泛函关系分析均支持这一结论。

Abstract: In this paper, using the example of a two-dimensional nonlinear sigma model with the Heisenberg group, we compare two variants of chiral field decomposition into a background part and a fluctuation. It is shown that only one of these methods is consistent with the construction of the generating functional by introducing a background field. Furthermore, we perform a one-loop renormalization of the quantum action, calculate power-law singularities in the two-loop approximation, and consider transition to an extended classical action. Finally, we study the consistency of the cutoff with special functional relations within the framework of the background field method.

</details>


### [131] [Bounds on nonlinear electrodynamics via resummed relative entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23323)
*Pietro Conzinu,Daiki Ueda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过背景电磁场中的相对熵研究非线性电动力学有效场论（EFT），证明相对熵可统一诊断微扰一致性和非微扰不稳定性：弱耦合下相对熵非负性对高阶算符系数施加符号约束，强耦合下相对熵违反可区分EFT截断失效（微扰分析）与物理不稳定性（如Schwinger效应，非微扰分析）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性电动力学EFT的约束多局限于领头阶系数（如标准正性界），缺乏系统工具来探测整个高阶算符塔的结构以及区分微扰一致性与非微扰稳定性问题。

Method: 在背景电磁场中计算相对熵，结合微扰分析、Borel-Laplace重求和、Schwinger本征时间法，研究费米子QED、标量QED和Dirac-Born-Infeld理论，并推广到具有阶乘或幂律增长的EFT系数的一般UV完备化理论。

Result: 1) 弱耦合下相对熵非负性对高阶算符的有限截断施加符号约束，推广了标准正性界；2) 强耦合下相对熵违反的起源不同：微扰分析中的违反标志截断失效，非微扰分析中的违反指示Schwinger效应等物理不稳定性；3) 通过重求和相对熵的非负性推导出对一般UV完备化理论的约束。

Conclusion: 相对熵为非线性EFT提供了一个统一诊断工具，可同时探测微扰一致性和非微扰稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate nonlinear electrodynamic effective field theories (EFTs) through the relative entropy evaluated in suitable background electromagnetic fields. In this setup, the relative entropy encodes information about the infinite tower of higher-dimensional operators and provides a systematic probe of nonlinear EFT effects. We study these features in fermionic QED, scalar QED, and Dirac-Born-Infeld theory using perturbative analyses, resummation techniques such as Borel--Laplace resummation, and non-perturbative approaches including the Schwinger proper-time method. In the weak-coupling regime, we show that the non-negativity of the perturbative relative entropy imposes sign constraints on finite truncations of higher-dimensional operators, generalizing familiar positivity bounds on leading EFT coefficients. We further show that violations of non-negativity in the strong-coupling regime admit qualitatively different interpretations depending on the framework: perturbatively analyzed violations diagnose the breakdown of the truncated EFT expansion, whereas violations in resummed or genuinely non-perturbative relative entropy signal physical instabilities of the system, such as the Schwinger effect. Extending the analysis to broader classes of UV completions, including theories with factorial or power-law growth of EFT coefficients, we derive general constraints on nonlinear electrodynamic EFT effects from the non-negativity of the resummed relative entropy. Our results suggest that relative entropy provides a unified diagnostic of perturbative consistency and non-perturbative stability in nonlinear EFTs.

</details>


### [132] [NLO Angular Impulse and Leading Singularities to all orders in spin for Kerr Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23450)
*Lucas Haiashi,Gabriel Menezes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用KMOC形式论和散射振幅方法，在次领头阶（NLO）计算了Kerr黑洞散射过程中的自旋依赖角动量冲量（自旋kick），得到了对所有自旋阶数有效的紧凑表达式，并通过多种一致性检验验证了结果正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于振幅的方法扩展到自旋相关的经典引力动力学，特别是计算Kerr黑洞散射中NLO自旋kick，以验证振幅方法在处理含自旋双体系统中的高效性和正确性。

Method: 采用KMOC形式论，基于在壳散射振幅和主导奇点，直接从量子振幅提取经典可观测量。通过自旋补充条件和自旋大小守恒进行一致性检验，并与径向作用结果进行对比，同时从三角形主导奇点提取非相对论引力势。

Result: 得到了对所有自旋阶数有效的自旋依赖角动量冲量紧凑表达式；保守结果在2PM阶保持协变自旋补充条件和自旋大小守恒；二次自旋保守结果与径向作用结果一致；从三角形主导奇点提取的引力势重现了已知的线性自旋-轨道相互作用。

Conclusion: 振幅方法在经典引力动力学中高效且有效，KMOC形式论是计算双黑洞散射中自旋依赖可观测量（如自旋kick）的强大框架。

Abstract: We compute the next-to-leading order (NLO) angular impulse (spin kick) in the scattering of Kerr black holes using the Kosower--Maybee--O'Connell (KMOC) formalism. Our approach is based on on-shell scattering amplitudes and leading singularities, allowing for a direct extraction of classical observables from quantum amplitudes. We derive compact expressions for the spin-dependent angular impulse valid to all orders in the spin variables at the integrand level, and show that these results reduce to known expressions in the appropriate limits. We perform detailed consistency checks: the conservative result preserves both the covariant spin supplementary condition and the spin magnitude through 2PM order, and the quadratic-in-spin conservative result agrees with existing radial-action results after translating between the direct KMOC spin kick and the radial-action observable. In addition, we extract the corresponding non-relativistic gravitational potential from the triangle leading singularities, obtaining a representation that resums spin effects and reproduces the known spin-orbit interaction at linear order. Our results provide further evidence for the efficiency of amplitude-based methods in classical gravitational dynamics, and highlight the KMOC formalism as a powerful framework for computing spin-dependent observables in binary black hole scattering.

</details>


### [133] [Geometric and Statistical Thermo Field Dynamics in de Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23484)
*D. S. Cabral,L. A. S. Evangelista,J. C. R. de Souza,A. F. Santos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小耦合标量场在膨胀de Sitter时空中的动力学，基于热场动力学（Thermo Field Dynamics）构建了统一描述视界诱导热效应与内禀热效应的理论框架，分析了不同观测者视角下的Bogoliubov变换与粒子产生，发现共动框架下的粒子数密度在辐射极限下守恒，与CMB热力学演化一致，而静态框架下有限温度效应会刺激Parker粒子产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在de Sitter时空中，共动观测者将Bunch-Davies态视为真空，而静态观测者则将其感知为Gibbons-Hawking温度下的热浴。这种观测者依赖性尚未得到统一的热力学描述，亟需建立一个能够同时涵盖视界诱导热效应与内禀热效应的理论框架。

Method: 利用Thermo Field Dynamics方法，将宇宙学视界相关的几何加倍与内禀热浴引起的统计加倍相结合，构建热力学的双重结构；通过分析Bogoliubov角的时域演化，分别计算共动系与静态系下的粒子数密度，并考察辐射极限、大质量及非最小耦合情形下的行为。

Result: 在辐射极限下，共动框架中的粒子数密度守恒，呈现出与CMB一致的热力学演化；在静态框架下，有限温度效应刺激Parker粒子产生。对于大质量非最小耦合场，几何温度与统计温度相互作用，产生特征热标度并对初始条件敏感。加倍结构揭示了时空全局因果结构与有限温度效应的深刻联系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的理论框架，可同时描述de Sitter时空中视界诱导的与内禀的热效应，揭示了Thermo Field Dynamics中的加倍结构并非纯数学技巧，而是时空因果结构与有限温度效应的物理体现。该框架为处理存在表观视界热效应与内禀热效应的量子场论问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The dynamics of a massive scalar field non-minimally coupled to gravity in an expanding de Sitter universe are investigated. It is shown that a comoving observer identifies the Bunch--Davies state as the vacuum, whereas a static observer perceives the same state as a thermal bath at the Gibbons--Hawking temperature. Motivated by this observer dependence, a thermal formulation based on Thermo Field Dynamics is developed by combining the geometric doubling associated with the cosmological horizon with the statistical doubling induced by a intrinsec thermal bath. The resulting construction reveals that the doubling procedure is not merely a mathematical artifact, but rather a manifestation of the global causal structure of spacetime together with finite-temperature effects. The temporal evolution of the Bogoliubov angle is analyzed and the corresponding particle number densities are evaluated in both comoving and static frames. In the radiation limit, the comoving number density remains conserved, providing a thermodynamic evolution consistent with that of the Cosmic Microwave Background, whereas in the static frame finite-temperature effects sti\-mulate Parker particle creation. For massive and non-minimally coupled fields, the interplay between geometric and statistical temperatures gives rise to a characteristic thermal scale and a nontrivial dependence on the initial conditions. These results provide a unified framework for describing quantum fields in de Sitter spacetime in the presence of both apparent horizon-induced and intrinsec thermal effects.

</details>


### [134] [Microscopic entropy of de Sitter spacetime and entropic solution to the old cosmological constant problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23522)
*Mariano Cadoni,Lorenzo Herres,Lorenzo Orlando,Mirko Pitzalis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了共形引力中Weyl对称性破缺与爱因斯坦引力中剩余标度对称性的关系，提出耦合常数α可解释为德西特时空的贝肯斯坦-霍金熵。通过泛函重整化群、全息原理和涌现引力，将α视为德西特视界微观自由度的度量。α(k)的重整化群流在红外方向单调递增要求导致与观测值同量级的宇宙学常数，从而为宇宙学常数问题提供了一个熵解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决宇宙学常数问题（观测值极小但理论预估值极大的矛盾），探索Weyl对称性破缺在共形引力与爱因斯坦引力之间的桥梁作用，并为耦合常数α提供微观解释。

Method: 结合泛函重整化群（FRG）、全息原理（holography）和涌现引力（emergent gravity）的框架，将α解释为德西特视界微观自由度；通过分析α(k)的重整化群流及其红外单调递增性质来约束宇宙学常数的量级。

Result: 发现α可自然解释为德西特时空的贝肯斯坦-霍金熵；重正化群流的红外单调性要求导致宇宙学常数与观测值同量级，即极小的宇宙学常数源于德西特宇宙中极大的微观自由度数。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于熵的宇宙学常数问题解决方案，将看似微小的宇宙学常数归因于德西特视界背后巨大的微观自由度，为老宇宙学常数问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the role of Weyl symmetry breaking in conformal gravity and the residual scale symmetry of Einstein gravity. The corresponding action is characterized by a dimensionless coupling $α$, determined by the ratio between the de Sitter and Planck scales. We show that this quantity admits a natural interpretation as the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy of de Sitter spacetime. Combining ideas from the functional renormalization group, holography, and emergent gravity, we propose a microscopic interpretation of $α$ as a measure of the degrees of freedom associated with the de Sitter horizon. In this framework, the renormalization group flow of $α(k)$ encodes the scale dependence of these microscopic degrees of freedom. Requiring this flow to be monotonically increasing toward the infrared leads to a cosmological constant of the same order as the observed one, suggesting an entropic solution to the old cosmological constant problem. This remarkably small value can therefore be understood as a direct consequence of the extraordinarily large number of degrees of freedom in our de Sitter universe.

</details>


### [135] [One-loop contributions in Lorentz-violating scalar QED with nonminimal coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23523)
*F. M. Belchior,J. R. Nascimento,A. Yu. Petrov,P. Porfirio*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在微扰框架下研究了包含维度五CPT奇非最小耦合的Lorentz破缺标量QED模型中的单圈两点函数发散结构，证明了规范不变性保持，并提取了Lorentz破缺的有效场论对抵消项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lorentz破缺理论的微扰方面，研究包含维度五CPT奇非最小耦合的Lorentz破缺标量QED扩展模型中单圈两点函数的发散结构，以检验规范不变性是否保持，并理解非最小耦合在有效场论框架下的行为。

Method: 通过包含常数背景矢量和对偶电磁场强度的广义协变导数定义模型，展开作用量后导出光子和标量自能所需的全部顶点（包括混合最小-非最小海鸥图顶点）。采用维数正规化方法计算真空极化和标量自能的发散部分。

Result: 完整的光子两点函数是横向的，因为混合和纯非最小泡泡图产生的质量关联项被对应的海鸥图抵消，留下更高阶导数的CPT奇和CPT偶LV规范抵消项。标量自能生成LV动能和更高阶导数抵消项。

Conclusion: 计算证实规范不变性得以保持，非最小相互作用应在有效场论展开框架下理解。

Abstract: In the context of pertubative aspects of Lorentz-violating theories, we study the divergent one-loop two-point functions of a Lorentz-violating (LV) extension of scalar QED containing a dimension-five CPT-odd nonminimal coupling. The model is defined through a generalized covariant derivative involving a constant background vector and the dual electromagnetic field strength. After expanding the action, we derive the full set of vertices relevant for the photon and scalar self-energies, including the mixed minimal-nonminimal seagull interaction diagram required by the gauge invariance. Using dimensional regularization, we compute the divergent parts of the vacuum polarization and scalar self-energy. The complete photon two-point function is shown to be transverse since the mass-dependent pieces generated by the mixed and purely nonminimal bubble graphs cancel against the corresponding seagull graphs, leaving higher-derivative CPT-odd and CPT-even LV gauge counterterms. The scalar self-energy generates LV kinetic and higher-derivative counterterms. The calculation confirms that gauge invariance is preserved, while the nonminimal interaction should be understood within an effective-field-theory expansion.

</details>


### [136] [Semiclassical decay of de Sitter space into black holes with vortex-deformed horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23624)
*Andrés Gomberoff,Carla Henríquez-Baez,Aldo Vera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了de Sitter空间通过BPS涡旋（$\mathrm{CP}^1$作用）衰变为黑洞的过程，该过程由涡旋推广的Nariai瞬子介导，其欧几里得几何为$S^2\timesΣ$，衰变率由离散拓扑荷控制，揭示了物质涡旋为de Sitter空间开辟了新的拓扑组织的衰变通道家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索de Sitter空间通过带有BPS涡旋（$\mathrm{CP}^1$作用）的黑洞进行衰变的机制，理解物质涡旋如何影响de Sitter空间的衰变过程。

Method: 构造了一个涡旋变形的Nariai瞬子作为正则欧几里得瞬子，该瞬子的几何结构为$S^2\timesΣ$，其中$Σ$是一个紧致曲面，其几何由涡旋构型塑造。

Result: 发现衰变率由离散拓扑荷控制，表明物质涡旋为de Sitter空间开辟了新的、按拓扑性质组织的衰变通道家族。

Conclusion: 物质（BPS涡旋）的存在为de Sitter空间的衰变引入了新的拓扑自由度，丰富了其衰变通道，并可通过离散拓扑荷对衰变率进行分类和控制。

Abstract: We study the decay of de Sitter space into black holes whose horizons are dressed by BPS vortices of a $\mathrm{CP}^1$ action. The process is mediated by a regular Euclidean instanton obtained as a vortex-deformed generalization of the Nariai instanton. Its Euclidean geometry has the form $S^2\timesΣ$, where $Σ$ is a compact surface whose geometry is shaped by the vortex configuration. The resulting decay rates are controlled by a discrete topological charge, showing that matter vortices open a new topologically organized family of decay channels for de Sitter space.

</details>
